SELECT `p`.`appointment_id` AS `Id`, COALESCE((
    SELECT `d0`.`doctor_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p0`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d0` ON `p0`.`appointment_id` = `d0`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p0`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p0`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1), 0) AS `doctor`, (
    SELECT `d1`.`bed_id`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p1`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d1` ON `p1`.`appointment_id` = `d1`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p1`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p1`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `bed`, (
    SELECT `d2`.`nurse_assistant`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p2`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d2` ON `p2`.`appointment_id` = `d2`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p2`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p2`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `nurse`, (
    SELECT `d3`.`procedure`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p3`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d3` ON `p3`.`appointment_id` = `d3`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p3`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p3`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `procedure`, (
    SELECT `d4`.`status`
    FROM `p_appointments` AS `p4`
    INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d4` ON `p4`.`appointment_id` = `d4`.`appointment_id`
    WHERE ((EXTRACT(month FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p4`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)) AND (`p`.`appointment_id` = `p4`.`appointment_id`)
    LIMIT 1) AS `status`
FROM `p_appointments` AS `p`
INNER JOIN `duty_assignments` AS `d` ON `p`.`appointment_id` = `d`.`appointment_id`
WHERE (EXTRACT(month FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__month_0) AND (EXTRACT(year FROM `p`.`appointment_date`) = @__year_1)
GROUP BY `p`.`appointment_id`
